- **incident-command-and-review**: 建立只读事件态势、明确指挥角色、组织决策节奏、定义恢复标准，并在稳定后完成无责复盘与纠正措施复验。用于服务异常、业务中断、安全或数据事件的协调与复盘；不用于直接修改生产环境、部署修复、使用凭证、对外通知、替技术负责人诊断实现，或未经人类批准执行任何高风险动作。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/tree/HEAD/skills/incident-command-and-review))

- **incident-commander-playbook**: Use when performing incident commander playbook — comprehensive incident commander (IC) playbook covering IC responsibilities, communication cadence, escalation decision frameworks, delegation patterns, and handoff procedures. ([cloudthinker-ai/CloudSkills](https://github.com/cloudthinker-ai/CloudSkills/tree/HEAD/skills/templates/incident-commander-playbook) · ★7)
- **incident-commander-prefer-the-move-you-can-undo**: When choosing between actions under pressure, prefer the one that can be undone quickly, and do what is needed beforehand (capturing evidence, rehearsing the rollback) so that undoing stays cheap. Use when a mitigation is available and the cause is not yet known; a tempting action cannot be reversed, such as deleting data; a launch relies on a rollback nobody has run. ([Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper](https://github.com/Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper/tree/HEAD/examples/incident-commander/skills/incident-commander-prefer-the-move-you-can-undo) · ★10)
- **incident-commander-price-the-risk-offer-a-cheaper-path**: When someone wants to take a risk to meet a goal, do not simply refuse: state what the risk would cost and who would pay it, then offer ways to reach the goal at lower cost. Use when pressure to skip a safeguard to hold a date; a request that would spend other people's trust; a fixed external commitment conflicts with a safe plan. ([Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper](https://github.com/Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper/tree/HEAD/examples/incident-commander/skills/incident-commander-price-the-risk-offer-a-cheaper-path) · ★10)
- **incident-commander-promise-the-next-update-not-the-fix**: During an incident, commit to when people will next hear from you rather than when the problem will be fixed, and never adopt another party's estimate as your own commitment. Use when people affected by an incident are asking when it will be fixed; a vendor or another team has given a fix estimate; someone proposes telling customers a resolution time. ([Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper](https://github.com/Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper/tree/HEAD/examples/incident-commander/skills/incident-commander-promise-the-next-update-not-the-fix) · ★10)
- **incident-commander-reasoning**: How the incident commander approaches problems in site reliability and incident command. Start here for any situation in that field: it says which reasoning patterns to activate and in what order. ([Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper](https://github.com/Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper/tree/HEAD/examples/incident-commander/skills/incident-commander-reasoning) · ★10)
- **incident-commander-role**: Operate as an incident commander who owns the response to a live outage: severity, coordination, and communication, not the fix itself. Use when a production incident is active and someone must run the room instead of everyone debugging in parallel. ([Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS](https://github.com/Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS/tree/HEAD/skills/big-tech-roles/incident-commander-role) · ★14)
- **incident-commander-size-the-response-to-harm-and-reversibility**: Decide how much process, escalation and review a situation deserves from how many people are being hurt and whether the harm can be undone - and look harder whenever a small problem recurs. Use when deciding whether something is an incident at all; deciding how many people to page; deciding how large a post-incident review should be; a small event has happened before. ([Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper](https://github.com/Tucuxi-Inc/PublicReasoningMapper/tree/HEAD/examples/incident-commander/skills/incident-commander-size-the-response-to-harm-and-reversibility) · ★10)
- **incident-commander-stabilise-first-diagnose-second**: While people are being harmed, stop the harm before finding the cause; schedule diagnosis for afterwards, using evidence captured before mitigating. Use when customers are failing now and an engineer wants to investigate live; a cheap mitigation exists before the cause is known.
